### 5.43 Built-in lists (LST) — UC-41

*Group rationale: the product owner (25 Nov 2026): "I would like to use the QE list from Azure to map in other tables. Maybe it should be saved as a list and then used? This will go into Field Type - Dropdown (in-built List)." Their answers: the QE list is the members of an Azure DevOps team (names and emails); on keeping emails in Git (26 Nov 2026) the key comes "From ADO": people are keyed by their Azure DevOps identity id and no email is saved; the saved list stays current with a Refresh button and is saved in Git as a shared list — an owner clicks "Refresh from Azure DevOps" and sees what is added and removed before saving; values already used in rows are kept and marked when they leave the list; the Tool Owner, the Admin, the Scrum Master and the owners of On-Demand pages create and refresh the lists. Reuse: the Epic setup's **Value lists** (EPS-02) already are the manual shared lists, so the built-in lists are a new store for team lists only (shared-lists.json, not inside epic-setup.json, whose reader would drop the source, the emails and the log in an older app), managed in the same place (Admin → Epic & Feature setup → Value lists) and offered together with the Value lists in one catalog; People & access's team read (`fetchTeamMembers`, CAP-51) is the read used; the column types are the existing Dropdown and Multi-select with a list reference, so filters, Insights, Replace and the bulk upload work as they do for options.*

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. | Trace |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| LST-01 | **Built-in lists** shall be shared lists of names that the On-Demand table pages use as the choices of a column: **team lists** — the members of an Azure DevOps team — kept in the Git data repository (LST-02), and the Epic setup's **Value lists** (EPS-02) as they are. A list is referred to by a column as `team:<id>` or `values:<id>`. | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-02 | The team lists shall be one shared file, `shared-lists.json`, next to the planning data in the Git data repository (never in the app's repository, never with a PAT), stored like the other shared setup: read when Git storage is connected and kept in this browser as the last good copy for that repository, branch and data folder. Changing workspace shall show only its own cached or fetched lists; saved **queued and merged** with what others saved meanwhile (GIT-17: lists by id, the later save wins where both changed one list, each list's log the union of both); read **strictly** (a file that is not the file, a list without a valid id, name or https source, or a list twice is refused as a whole with the reason, and the lists in use stay as they were); watched for **newer data in Git** (loaded quietly on every page, the notice only on Admin → Epic & Feature setup); never written with synthetic sample data. Before writing this file, the app shall verify that the configured Git repository is private. | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-03 | Each team list shall have a **name** (unique, any case; at most 60 characters), a **source** — the Azure DevOps **server** (dev.azure.com/<organization> or an Azure DevOps Server collection, https, no credentials), **project** and **team** — its **items**, who created it and when, when and by whom it was **last refreshed**, and a **log** (LST-05); at most 50 lists. | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-04 | A list's **items** shall be the team members' **display names** and, for a person, their **Azure DevOps identity id** (`identity.id` of the team-members read: a GUID, kept in lower case) — the key "from ADO" (product owner, 26 Nov 2026); each id once, a name without an id once; groups are left out (as People & access reads the team, CAP-51); sorted by name; at most 1,000. **No email** is kept in the file or in the page and row files — not as a key, not as who created or refreshed a list (a display name, else "someone"); the emails the read returns are shown in the Refresh pop-up only and kept in memory for it. | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-05 | Each list shall keep a **log** of its last 50 changes — who, when (UTC), what (Created, Refreshed, Edited) and the names added, removed and renamed ("old → new"), up to 20 each, and what an edit changed — shown with **Log** to everyone. | T | S | UC-41 |
| LST-06 | **↻ Refresh…** (and the first read of ＋ New list…, **↻ Read the team**) shall read the team's members from Azure DevOps — **read only**, the existing team-members read (CAP-30), with the viewer's **own PAT** for that server from this browser (the Time Writing connection's when it is that server); on-premises servers are read as well since nothing is written — and show, before anything is saved, the difference: **added**, **removed** and **renamed** (the same identity id, a new name), each with the email Azure DevOps gives as a hint on the screen, the number unchanged, and how many rows use a removed person. **Save to Git** saves the list with its items, the refresh time and person and one log entry; Cancel saves nothing. A server without a PAT in this browser is named with what to do. | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-07 | **Edit…** shall rename a list or change its source (logged as Edited; the next Refresh reads the new team); **Remove** shall remove a list that **no column uses** after a pop-up that names it (UI-STANDARDS §4). | T | S | UC-41 |
| LST-08 | **Set up columns…** (OND-05) shall offer the types **Dropdown (built-in list)** and **Multi-select (built-in list)** after the existing types; for them a **Built-in list** picker (the team lists and the Value lists, each with its size) replaces the options; the column is saved as a Dropdown or Multi-select with its list (`list`) and, as its options, the list's names at that time — so an app version without built-in lists still shows a Dropdown with those choices (LIM-105). A built-in list column needs its list. Everywhere the column is shown — the row form, the table, filters, Insights and dashboards (grouped by the display name), Replace and the bulk upload — its choices are **the list as it is now**; a list that is not found falls back to the options last saved, marked in the row form. | T | M | UC-37, UC-41 |
| LST-09 | A row shall store a built-in list value as the item's **display name** (in the cells, as every value) and, for a person, their **Azure DevOps identity id** as its stable key (the row's `refs`, one per value, in the page file or the row file); a value is shown with the name the list has now for its id, so a **renamed person still matches** (rows, filters, Insights); the file's name is updated the next time that row is saved. Keys are read only for a built-in list column, one per value, GUIDs only (an email is dropped); a merge keeps a value with its keys; changing a column's list or type drops its keys. | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-10 | **Replace** (OND-100…OND-102) and the **bulk upload** (OND-103…OND-108) shall go by the **display name** (as shown, LST-15) and take the key from the list as it is now: Replace finds a value by its current name and takes as the new value only a value of the list (else refused with the reason), the key following the new value; the Excel template's data validation is the list as it is now (a strict list for a Dropdown) with the prompt "Choose one value of the built-in list.", and an upload accepts only the list's values, each saved with its key. | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-11 | A value **no longer in the list** (a person removed from the team) shall be **kept** in the row and shown as **"not in the list"**, as a removed option is (OND-06); the Refresh pop-up says how many rows use a removed person before saving. | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-12 | Creating, refreshing and editing lists shall need **"Manage built-in lists"** (CON-115: Tool Owner, Admin and Scrum Master by default) **or** being a **co-owner of an On-Demand page** or of a group that takes added pages (PGB-08) — ownership is per page in menu-layout.json, not a role, so it is a second way in, as for setting up the page (pageRights); removing a list needs "Manage built-in lists" (a list may be used on pages the co-owner does not own). Fail closed: nothing without a roles file (CON-101), in View as, with synthetic sample data, without Git storage or with a read-only token, while the file is read or when it could not be used. | T | M | UC-22, UC-41 |
| LST-13 | Everyone shall see the team lists **read only** in **Admin → Epic & Feature setup → Value lists**, under the Value lists, in the panel **Team lists from Azure DevOps**: name, source, number of people, last refreshed (when and by whom), the columns that use it ("Page: Column") and the actions ↻ Refresh…, Edit…, Log and Remove; ＋ New list… as the panel's main action; a disabled action says why (a visible line, the row buttons' tooltips, UI-STANDARDS §3); Search settings finds it ("qe list", "team members"). | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-14 | Nothing shall be written to Azure DevOps by the built-in lists; writes go to the Git data repository only, as one commit per change named "Built-in lists: create / refresh / edit / remove {list} …", with the result under Background jobs (UI-STANDARDS §8). | T | M | UC-41 |
| LST-15 | Two or more members of a team list with the **same display name** shall stay **separate items** (by their identity ids) and be shown — in the row form, the table, filters, Insights, Replace and the Excel template — as **"Name (#3f2a)"**: the name and the first 4 characters of their identity id, more when those are the same too; a name held by one member is shown as it is. The label follows the id (a person whose twin leaves is shown by the plain name again, the row following by the id), and no email or other personal data is used to tell people apart. | T | M | UC-41 |

## 6. Non-functional requirements

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. |
|---|---|---|---|
| NFR-01 | The app shall function with no server component other than static hosting and the external systems in §1.2. | I | M |
| NFR-02 | The app shall keep at least 14,000 work items in IndexedDB and reload them. | T | M |
| NFR-03 | Requests to Azure DevOps from the desktop proxy shall time out after 45 s. Web update checks shall time out after 15 s. | T | S |
| NFR-04 | Every write to Azure DevOps shall be confirmed by reading the item back. When confirmation is not possible, the app shall say the write could not be confirmed and shall not retry automatically. | T | M |
| NFR-05 | PAT input fields shall be password fields, and the desktop proxy shall remove the PAT from any Azure DevOps error text it returns. The redaction shall apply to the PAT as sent (trimmed). | I, T | M |
| NFR-06 | The desktop window shall open at 1440 × 920 px and shall not be resizable below 1024 × 700 px. | I, D | S |
| NFR-07 | The source repository shall contain no production data, tokens or employee details. | I | M |
| NFR-08 | The web app's first download shall hold the shell and the start page only: every other page — and the project documents of the Documentation page — shall be loaded when first opened, with "Loading the page…" shown meanwhile. The main script shall stay under 1 MB (300 KB compressed); v7.43.0: 814 KB (237 KB) against 2.85 MB (830 KB) before. | T | S |
| NFR-09 | The built app (web and desktop) shall carry a **Content-Security-Policy**: scripts only from the app itself (and `file:` for the desktop app, which opens the page from disk), no inline or evaluated scripts, no plugins (`object-src 'none'`), no form posts, base URL fixed, frames only for the Microsoft sign-in, requests only to https hosts (Azure DevOps, on-premises servers, GitHub, Microsoft Graph), images also from data: and blob: URLs; video and audio only from the app's own files (`media-src 'self'`, DOC-13); inline style attributes allowed. The local development server does not add it. | T | S |

## 7. Interface requirements

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. |
|---|---|---|---|
| IF-01 | Azure DevOps: the app shall use REST API version 7.1 by default, or the connection's API version, falling back per DSK-05 in the desktop app. | T | M |
| IF-02 | Azure DevOps writes shall be limited to: creating Features, User Stories and Tasks (title, area, description, acceptance criteria, iteration, assigned to, target date, parent); Original Estimate, Completed Work and Tags on Tasks; State and Iteration Path on any item; the fields of the Edit form (BKL-20); and **adding a comment** to a work item's Discussion as text (escaped, BKL-20) — from the Edit form, ＋ User Story and ＋ Task, and the **PI review** of a Feature (PI Review, My PI Review and the Close pop-up, PIR-13, PIR-17, PIR-18, CON-113); and **replacing a PI review comment** the viewer wrote (Edit review… in My PI Review and PI Review, PIR-34). Other comments are never edited, and no comment is deleted by the app. | I, T | M |
| IF-03 | GitHub: the app shall use the repository contents API with a fine-grained token that has Contents: Read and write. | I | M |
| IF-04 | *(Retired — moved to the backlog: SharePoint is not functional; see PROJECT-PLAN.md → Backlog)* Microsoft Graph: the app shall use the `/v1.0/` endpoint for the SharePoint site's default document library. | I | M |
| IF-05 | SharePoint REST (desktop app only, KPT-02): one GET of a file's content (`/_api/web/GetFileByServerRelativeUrl('…')/$value`) in the app's SharePoint session, read only. | I | S |

## 8. Open issues

The open issues (OI-NN: as-built behaviour that is ambiguous, inconsistent or may not match intent, each needing a decision from the product owner) and the known bugs are kept in one register, [KNOWN-ISSUES.md](KNOWN-ISSUES.md). Requirement rows that have an open issue attached refer to it by ID. None of them is changed silently.

## 9. Traceability

The complete trace — use case → user requirement → system requirement → detailed requirement → test case → UAT day → software component — is generated from these documents by `node scripts/traceability.mjs` into [TRACEABILITY-MATRIX.md](TRACEABILITY-MATRIX.md), and `pnpm test` fails when the generated matrix is out of date or a requirement is not covered. The forward trace from requirement to test cases is also kept by hand in [TEST-PLAN.md §7](TEST-PLAN.md#7-requirements-coverage-matrix).
